- **filescom-expectations**: Expectations let your Files.com site define what “correct” file delivery looks like, continuously evaluate whether it happened, and keep history when it did not. ([Files-com/files-cli](https://github.com/Files-com/files-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/filescom-expectations) · ★47)

- **filescom-permissions**: A Permission object represents a grant of access permission on a specific Path to a User or Group. - **filescom-requests**: A Request is a file that *should* be uploaded by a specific user or group. ([Files-com/files-cli](https://github.com/Files-com/files-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/filescom-requests) · ★47)

- **filescom-secrets**: A Secret stores named, typed secret material for later use by features that reference the Secret by ID. ([Files-com/files-cli](https://github.com/Files-com/files-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/filescom-secrets) · ★47)
- **filescom-sessions**: A Session is an operation that allows you to make further API calls using our REST API or SDKs as a specific user. ([Files-com/files-cli](https://github.com/Files-com/files-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/filescom-sessions) · ★47)

- **filescom-sites**: A Site is the place you'll come to update site settings, as well as manage site-wide API keys. ([Files-com/files-cli](https://github.com/Files-com/files-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/filescom-sites) · ★47)

- **filescom-styles**: A Style is a custom set of branding that can be applied on a per-folder basis. ([Files-com/files-cli](https://github.com/Files-com/files-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/filescom-styles) · ★47)
- **filescom-sync-logs**: A SyncLog is an audit log for monitoring and troubleshooting operations made by Remote Server Sync. ([Files-com/files-cli](https://github.com/Files-com/files-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/filescom-sync-logs) · ★47)

- **filescom-usage-snapshots**: A UsageSnapshot is a site-wide utilization report of storage, bandwidth, APIs, and user accounts of your Files.com site for the given time period. ([Files-com/files-cli](https://github.com/Files-com/files-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/filescom-usage-snapshots) · ★47)

- **filescom-user-requests**: A UserRequest is an operation that allows anonymous users to place a request for access on the login screen to the site administrator. ([Files-com/files-cli](https://github.com/Files-com/files-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/filescom-user-requests) · ★47)
- **filescom-user-security-events**: A UserSecurityEvent is a log record for user security activity such as user lockouts. ([Files-com/files-cli](https://github.com/Files-com/files-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/filescom-user-security-events) · ★47)

- **filescom-users**: A User represents a human or system/service user with the ability to connect to Files.com via any of the available connectivity methods (unless restricted to specific protocols).
